### 👤 USER · 2026-09-11 17:00:45 · seq=?

# Contest Log Collector — openvela AI 大赛 AI Coding 日志归集

为 openvela AI 大赛提供 AI Coding 日志归集能力,**工作区感应、对话结束自动入仓**。

## 核心架构

```
       ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
       │ 门控: 当前目录向上能找到 .repo/ ?                    │
       │   否 → 不在 openvela 工作区 → 完全不采集             │
       │   是 → 在 openvela 工作区 → 进入采集                 │
       └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               │ (在工作区内)
                               ↓
       ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
       │ 对话结束自动写入: demo 仓 logs/<login>/              │
       │ 只写文件,不自动 commit/push                         │
       │ 选手 git push 时跟代码一起上 GitHub                  │
       └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**关键安全特性**: 用"是否在 openvela 工作区(`.repo/`)内"作为隐私门控 —— 选手在个人项目(工作区外)跟 AI 聊的对话**完全不采集**;只有在比赛工作区内的对话才会自动写入选手仓 `logs/`,且工具自身**永远不会 git push**,上传由选手控制。

## 自动入仓(默认,无需操作)

在 openvela 工作区内跟 AI 工具(Claude Code / OpenCode / Codex)对话,会话结束时日志会**自动写入**选手仓的 `logs/<github_login>/`。选手只需在提交代码时一起 push:

```bash
git add logs/
git commit -s -m "logs: capture session"
git push
```

## (可选)手动补导

正常流程不需要,但你可以用手动工具查看清单或补导某次会话。

### 1. 短命令 `contest-snapshot`(`install.sh` 装在 `~/.local/bin/`)

```bash
# 列出本机已采集的所有 session
contest-snapshot --list

# 补导某个 session (加 --confirm 真写入)
contest-snapshot --session <session-id> --confirm

# 按日期补导
contest-snapshot --today --confirm
contest-snapshot --since 2026-06-15 --confirm
```

### 2. 完整路径 fallback(`~/.local/bin` 不在 PATH 时)

```bash
python3 ../.claude/skills/contest-log-collector/tools/export-session.py --list
```

### 3. 自然语言 / Slash command

对 AI 说"补导这次会话",或在 Claude Code 里跑 `/contest-snapshot`,AI 会调用 `export-session.py` 预览,确认后加 `--confirm` 写入。

> ⚠️ 手动工具的 `--confirm`:不加时只预览不写文件。手动补导仅用于补救/管理,正常对话已自动入仓。

## 目录结构

```
contest-log-collector/
├── SKILL.md                       # 本文件
├── commands/
│   └── contest-snapshot.md        # slash command 定义
├── onboarding/
│   ├── install.sh                 # 主入口
│   ├── verify-setup.sh            # 健康检查
│   ├── JUDGE_GUIDE.md             # 评委指南 (装到选手仓根)
│   └── USAGE.md                   # 选手手册 (装到选手仓根)
├── adapters/
│   ├── opencode/collector.js      # OpenCode V1 plugin
│   ├── shared/                    # Claude Code + Codex 共用
│   │   ├── snapshot_core.py
│   │   ├── get_github_login.py
│   │   └── get_github_login.js
├── schema/
│   ├── event.schema.json
│   └── manifest.schema.json
└── tools/
    ├── export-session.py          # 选手主动导出工具 (核心)
    ├── render-log.py              # 评委渲染工具
    └── validate-log.py            # 防作弊校验
```

## 组委会预装(建仓时)

```bash
git clone --depth 1 -b dev-ai-contest-2026 \
  https://github.com/open-vela/.claude.git /tmp/clt
cd <选手仓路径>
bash /tmp/clt/skills/contest-log-collector/onboarding/install.sh \
  --team-id contest2026-042 \
  --github-login <login>
git add -A && git commit -m "init: contest-log-collector preinstalled"
git push
```

`install.sh` 安装项:

- **本机**:
  - `~/.claude/contest-collector.env` — 选手身份(TEAM_ID + GITHUB_LOGIN)
  - `~/.claude/contest-shared/` — snapshot core + 全局 hook 脚本
  - `~/.claude/settings.json` — 注册 Stop / SessionEnd hook(deep merge,保留原有配置)
  - `~/.claude/contest-collector-staging/` — staging 区
- **选手仓**:
  - `.claude/shared/` — snapshot core(给 export 工具用)
  - `.claude/commands/contest-snapshot.md` — slash command
  - `.opencode/plugins/contest-collector.js` — OpenCode plugin
  - `../.claude/skills/contest-log-collector/tools/export-session.py` + `render-log.py` + `validate-log.py`
  - `schema/event.schema.json` + `manifest.schema.json`
  - `JUDGE_GUIDE.md` — 评委指南
  - `USAGE.md` — 选手手册
  - `.gitignore` — 已加日志相关排除

## 数据契约

JSONL 每条事件必含字段:

- `schema_version` — 当前 1.0
- `session_id` — AI 工具内部 session ID
- `team_id` — 对应仓库的官方匿名编号
- `github_login` — 当前组员 GitHub username
- `tool` — opencode / claude-code / codex / kiro
- `seq` — session 内单调递增(防作弊核心)
- `ts` — ISO 8601 UTC 时间戳
- `role` — user / assistant / tool / system

## 安全特性

- **工作区门控**: 只在 openvela 工作区(向上能找到 `.repo/`)内采集,工作区外的对话(含个人项目)完全不采集
- **不自动上传**: 自动入仓只写本机仓内 `logs/` 文件,工具自身从不 `git push`,上传由选手控制
- **自动脱敏**: 默认正则覆盖 `sk-*` (API key) / `ghp_*` (GitHub token) / `Bearer *` (auth header)
- **TEAM_ID 缺失主动拒绝**: 防止数据归属错乱
- **多人组天然安全**: 按 `<github_login>/` 一人一目录,组员之间互不干扰

## 工具兼容性

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""
3	Export AI Coding sessions from staging into the demo repo's logs/ directory.
4	
5	Staging path:  ~/.claude/contest-collector-staging/<github_login>/<date>/<tool>__<sid>.jsonl
6	Target path:   <repo_root>/logs/<github_login>/<date>/<tool>__<sid>.jsonl
7	
8	Normally sessions auto-export into the demo repo's logs/ at session end
9	(see the adapters). This script is the manual path: list, re-export, or
10	selectively export staged sessions.
11	
12	Usage:
13	  python3 tools/export-session.py --latest
14	  python3 tools/export-session.py --session <session-id>
15	  python3 tools/export-session.py --since <date>
16	  python3 tools/export-session.py --all
17	  python3 tools/export-session.py --list
18	"""
19	from __future__ import annotations
20	
21	import argparse
22	import json
23	import shutil
24	import subprocess
25	import sys
26	from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
27	from pathlib import Path
28	
29	
30	def staging_root() -> Path:
31	    return Path.home() / ".claude" / "contest-collector-staging"
32	
33	
34	def _load_env_github_login() -> str | None:
35	    env_file = Path.home() / ".claude" / "contest-collector.env"
36	    if not env_file.is_file():
37	        return None
38	    try:
39	        for line in env_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
40	            if line.startswith("GITHUB_LOGIN="):
41	                val = line.split("=", 1)[1].strip().strip('"').strip("'")
42	                return val or None
43	    except Exception:
44	        return None
45	    return None
46	
47	
48	def repo_root() -> Path | None:
49	    try:
50	        out = subprocess.check_output(
51	            ["git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"],
52	            stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True,
53	        )
54	        return Path(out.strip())
55	    except Exception:
56	        return None
57	
58	
59	def list_sessions(github_login: str | None) -> list[dict]:
60	    root = staging_root()
61	    if not root.exists():
62	        return []
63	    if github_login is None:
64	        env_login = _load_env_github_login()
65	        if env_login:
66	            github_login = env_login
67	    sessions = []
68	    for member_dir in sorted(root.iterdir()):
69	        if not member_dir.is_dir() or member_dir.name == "errors":
70	            continue
71	        if github_login and member_dir.name != github_login:
72	            continue
73	        manifest_path = member_dir / "manifest.json"
74	        if not manifest_path.exists():
75	            continue
76	        try:
77	            manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
78	        except Exception:
79	            continue
80	        for s in manifest.get("sessions", []):
81	            sessions.append({
82	                "github_login": member_dir.name,
83	                "session_id": s.get("session_id"),
84	                "tool": s.get("tool"),
85	                "started_at": s.get("started_at", ""),
86	                "last_event_at": s.get("last_event_at", ""),
87	                "event_count": s.get("event_count", 0),
88	                "file_path": s.get("file_path", ""),
89	                "model": s.get("model", ""),
90	            })
91	    return sessions
92	
93	
94	def filter_sessions(sessions: list[dict], args) -> list[dict]:
95	    if args.session:
96	        return [s for s in sessions if s["session_id"] == args.session]
97	    if args.latest:
98	        if not sessions:
99	            return []
100	        return [max(sessions, key=lambda s: s.get("last_event_at") or "")]
101	    if args.since:
102	        try:
103	            cutoff = datetime.fromisoformat(args.since.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
104	        except ValueError:
105	            cutoff = datetime.combine(
106	                datetime.strptime(args.since, "%Y-%m-%d").date(),
107	                datetime.min.time(), tzinfo=timezone.utc,
108	            )
109	        if cutoff.tzinfo is None:
110	            cutoff = cutoff.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
111	        result = []
112	        for s in sessions:
113	            ts = s.get("last_event_at") or s.get("started_at") or ""
114	            try:
115	                ev_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
116	                if ev_dt.tzinfo is None:
117	                    ev_dt = ev_dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
118	                if ev_dt >= cutoff:
119	                    result.append(s)
120	            except ValueError:
121	                continue
122	        return result
123	    if args.today:
124	        today = datetime.now(timezone.utc).date()
125	        result = []
126	        for s in sessions:
127	            ts = s.get("last_event_at") or s.get("started_at") or ""
128	            try:
129	                ev_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
130	                if ev_dt.date() == today:
131	                    result.append(s)
132	            except ValueError:
133	                continue
134	        return result
135	    if args.all:
136	        return sessions
137	    return []
138	
139	
140	def copy_session(s: dict, dest_repo: Path, dry_run: bool) -> tuple[bool, str]:
141	    src_root = staging_root() / s["github_login"]
142	    src_jsonl = src_root / s["file_path"].split(f"{s['github_login']}/", 1)[-1] \
143	        if "logs/" in s["file_path"] else None
144	    if src_jsonl is None or not src_jsonl.exists():
145	        date_part, fname = None, None
146	        if s["file_path"].count("/") >= 2:
147	            parts = s["file_path"].split("/")
148	            date_part, fname = parts[-2], parts[-1]
149	        if date_part and fname:
150	            src_jsonl = src_root / date_part / fname
151	    if not src_jsonl or not src_jsonl.exists():
152	        return False, f"source jsonl not found for session {s['session_id']}"
153	
154	    rel_inside = src_jsonl.relative_to(src_root)
155	    dest_jsonl = dest_repo / "logs" / s["github_login"] / rel_inside
156	
157	    if dry_run:
158	        return True, f"[dry-run] would copy {src_jsonl} -> {dest_jsonl}"
159	
160	    dest_jsonl.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
161	    shutil.copy2(src_jsonl, dest_jsonl)
162	
163	    actual_count = sum(1 for line in dest_jsonl.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if line.strip())
164	    s["event_count"] = actual_count
165	    s["file_path"] = f"logs/{s['github_login']}/{rel_inside}"
166	
167	    update_dest_manifest(s, dest_repo)
168	    return True, f"copied -> {dest_jsonl.relative_to(dest_repo)} ({actual_count} events)"
169	
170	
171	def update_dest_manifest(s: dict, dest_repo: Path) -> None:
172	    member_dir = dest_repo / "logs" / s["github_login"]
173	    manifest_path = member_dir / "manifest.json"
174	    if manifest_path.exists():
175	        manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
176	    else:
177	        src_manifest_path = staging_root() / s["github_login"] / "manifest.json"
178	        src_manifest = json.loads(src_manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
179	        manifest = {
180	            "schema_version": src_manifest.get("schema_version", "1.0"),
181	            "team_id": src_manifest.get("team_id", ""),
182	            "github_login": s["github_login"],
183	            "generator": src_manifest.get("generator", ""),
184	            "sessions": [],
185	        }
186	    existing = next((x for x in manifest["sessions"]
187	                     if x.get("session_id") == s["session_id"]), None)
188	    src_manifest_path = staging_root() / s["github_login"] / "manifest.json"
189	    src_manifest = json.loads(src_manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
190	    src_entry = next((x for x in src_manifest.get("sessions", [])
191	                      if x.get("session_id") == s["session_id"]), None)
192	    if src_entry is None:
193	        return
194	    entry = dict(src_entry)
195	    if "event_count" in s:
196	        entry["event_count"] = s["event_count"]
197	    if "file_path" in s:
198	        entry["file_path"] = s["file_path"]
199	    if existing:
200	        existing.update(entry)
201	    else:
202	        manifest["sessions"].append(entry)
203	    manifest["updated_at"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
204	    manifest_path.write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False),
205	                             encoding="utf-8")
206	
207	
208	def cmd_list(args, sessions: list[dict]) -> int:
209	    if not sessions:
210	        print("No sessions in staging.")
211	        print(f"Staging path: {staging_root()}")
212	        return 0
213	    print(f"{'session_id':<40} {'tool':<12} {'last_event_at':<26} "
214	          f"{'events':>6}  model")
215	    print("-" * 100)
216	    for s in sorted(sessions, key=lambda x: x.get("last_event_at") or ""):
217	        sid = (s["session_id"] or "")[:38]
218	        tool = s.get("tool", "")[:10]
219	        last = s.get("last_event_at", "")[:25]
220	        cnt = s.get("event_count", 0)
221	        model = s.get("model", "")
222	        print(f"{sid:<40} {tool:<12} {last:<26} {cnt:>6}  {model}")
223	    return 0
224	
225	
226	def cmd_backfill(args) -> int:
227	    dest = Path(args.dest) if args.dest else repo_root()
228	    if dest is None:
229	        sys.stderr.write("Cannot detect demo repo. "
230	                         "Run inside the repo or pass --dest.\n")
231	        return 2
232	
233	    if args.force:
234	        staging = staging_root()
235	        if staging.exists():
236	            print(f"[--force] wiping staging at {staging}")
237	            shutil.rmtree(staging)
238	
239	    claude_projects = Path.home() / ".claude" / "projects"
240	    if not claude_projects.is_dir():
241	        sys.stderr.write(f"No Claude Code history found at {claude_projects}\n")
242	        return 1
243	
244	    skill_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
245	    core_py = skill_root / "adapters" / "shared" / "snapshot_core.py"
246	    if not core_py.exists():
247	        sys.stderr.write(f"Cannot find snapshot_core.py at {core_py}\n")
248	        return 1
249	
250	    transcripts = sorted(claude_projects.glob("*/*.jsonl"))
251	    if not transcripts:
252	        sys.stderr.write("No transcript files found in Claude Code history.\n")
253	        return 0
254	
255	    env_vars = subprocess.check_output(
256	        ["bash", "-c",
257	         "source ~/.claude/contest-collector.env 2>/dev/null && "
258	         "echo \"TEAM_ID=${TEAM_ID:-}\" && echo \"GITHUB_LOGIN=${GITHUB_LOGIN:-}\""],
259	        text=True,
260	    ).strip()
261	    env_map = dict(line.split("=", 1) for line in env_vars.splitlines() if "=" in line)
262	    team_id = env_map.get("TEAM_ID", "").strip()
263	    github_login = env_map.get("GITHUB_LOGIN", "").strip()
264	    if not team_id:
265	        sys.stderr.write("TEAM_ID not found. Run install.sh first.\n")
266	        return 2
267	    if not github_login:
268	        sys.stderr.write("GITHUB_LOGIN not found in ~/.claude/contest-collector.env. "
269	                         "Re-run install.sh with --github-login <your-name>.\n")
270	        return 2
271	
272	    print(f"Found {len(transcripts)} transcript(s) in Claude Code history.")
273	    print(f"Destination: {dest}/logs/")
274	    print(f"GitHub login: {github_login}")
275	    print()
276	
277	    child_env = {**subprocess.os.environ, "TEAM_ID": team_id,
278	                 "GITHUB_LOGIN": github_login, "CWD": str(dest)}
279	
280	    success = 0
281	    skipped = 0
282	    for t in transcripts:
283	        sid = t.stem
284	        payload = json.dumps({
285	            "session_id": sid,
286	            "cwd": str(dest),
287	            "transcript_path": str(t),
288	            "hook_event_name": "SessionEnd",
289	        })
290	        try:
291	            result = subprocess.run(
292	                [sys.executable, str(core_py), "--tool", "claude-code"],
293	                input=payload, text=True, capture_output=True,
294	                env=child_env,
295	                cwd=str(dest),
296	            )
297	            if result.returncode == 0:
298	                if "captured" in result.stderr:
299	                    success += 1
300	                    print(f"  ✅ {sid[:12]}  {result.stderr.strip().split('-> ')[-1] if '-> ' in result.stderr else 'ok'}")
301	                else:
302	                    skipped += 1
303	            else:
304	                print(f"  ⚠️  {sid[:12]}  {result.stderr.strip()[:80]}")
305	        except Exception as e:
306	            print(f"  ❌ {sid[:12]}  {e}")
307	
308	    print(f"\nBackfill done: {success} imported, {skipped} skipped (already captured or empty).")
309	    if success > 0:
310	        print("Next: git add logs/ && git commit -s -m 'logs: backfill history' && git push")
311	    return 0
312	
313	
314	def main() -> int:
315	    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
316	        description="Export AI Coding sessions from staging to demo repo logs/.",
317	        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
318	    )
319	    p.add_argument("--list", action="store_true",
320	                   help="List all sessions in staging without exporting.")
321	    p.add_argument("--latest", action="store_true",
322	                   help="Export only the most recent session.")
323	    p.add_argument("--session", metavar="SID",
324	                   help="Export a specific session by id.")
325	    p.add_argument("--today", action="store_true",
326	                   help="Export sessions from today (UTC).")
327	    p.add_argument("--since", metavar="DATE",
328	                   help="Export sessions since a date (YYYY-MM-DD).")
329	    p.add_argument("--all", action="store_true",
330	                   help="Export every session in staging.")
331	    p.add_argument("--backfill", action="store_true",
332	                   help="Scan Claude Code history (~/.claude/projects/) and "
333	                        "re-process all transcripts. Recovers sessions that "
334	                        "were created before the hook was installed.")
335	    p.add_argument("--force", action="store_true",
336	                   help="With --backfill, wipe the local staging first so all "
337	                        "transcripts are re-processed even if they were "
338	                        "captured before. Useful after cleaning logs/ to "
339	                        "rebuild from scratch.")
340	    p.add_argument("--github-login", metavar="LOGIN",
341	                   help="Restrict to one member's sessions.")
342	    p.add_argument("--dest", metavar="PATH",
343	                   help="Destination repo path. Defaults to git rev-parse --show-toplevel.")
344	    p.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true",
345	                   help="Force preview mode, do not write.")
346	    p.add_argument("--confirm", action="store_true",
347	                   help="Required to actually export. Without it, the tool only previews.")
348	    args = p.parse_args()
349	
350	    sessions = list_sessions(args.github_login)
351	
352	    if args.list:
353	        return cmd_list(args, sessions)
354	
355	    if args.backfill:
356	        return cmd_backfill(args)
357	
358	    if not any([args.latest, args.session, args.today, args.since, args.all]):
359	        p.print_help()
360	        sys.stderr.write("\nNothing to do. Pick one of "
361	                         "--latest, --session, --today, --since, --all, --list, --backfill.\n")
362	        return 2
363	
364	    selected = filter_sessions(sessions, args)
365	    if not selected:
366	        sys.stderr.write("No sessions matched.\n")
367	        return 1
368	
369	    dest = Path(args.dest) if args.dest else repo_root()
370	    if dest is None:
371	        sys.stderr.write("Cannot detect demo repo. "
372	                         "Run inside the repo or pass --dest.\n")
373	        return 2
374	
375	    preview_only = args.dry_run or not args.confirm
376	    rc = 0
377	    for s in selected:
378	        ok, msg = copy_session(s, dest, preview_only)
379	        prefix = "✅" if ok else "❌"
380	        print(f"{prefix} {s['session_id'][:12]}  {msg}")
381	        if not ok:
382	            rc = 1
383	
384	    if preview_only:
385	        print(f"\n[preview] {len(selected)} session(s) shown above. "
386	              "Nothing was written.")
387	        print("Re-run with --confirm to actually export, e.g.:")
388	        if args.latest:
389	            print("  python3 tools/export-session.py --latest --confirm")
390	        elif args.session:
391	            print(f"  python3 tools/export-session.py --session {args.session} --confirm")
392	        elif args.today:
393	            print("  python3 tools/export-session.py --today --confirm")
394	        elif args.since:
395	            print(f"  python3 tools/export-session.py --since {args.since} --confirm")
396	        elif args.all:
397	            print("  python3 tools/export-session.py --all --confirm")
398	    else:
399	        print(f"\n{len(selected)} session(s) exported to {dest}/logs/")
400	        print("Next: git add logs/ && git commit && git push")
401	
402	    return rc
403	
404	
405	if __name__ == "__main__":
406	    sys.exit(main())

63	def report_error(log_root: Path, kind: str, ctx: dict, exc: BaseException | str) -> None:
64	    try:
65	        errors_dir = log_root / "errors"
66	        errors_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
67	        err_path = errors_dir / f"{fs_timestamp()}_{kind}.err"
68	        payload = {
69	            "ts": iso_now(),
70	            "kind": kind,
71	            "tool": ctx.get("tool", "unknown"),
72	            "team_id": os.environ.get("TEAM_ID", ""),
73	            "version": VERSION,

75	            "message": str(exc),
76	        }
77	        err_path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
78	    except Exception:

80	    sys.stderr.write(
81	        f"[session-log] ERROR kind={kind} tool={ctx.get('tool','?')} "
82	        f"team={os.environ.get('TEAM_ID','')}: {exc}\n"

86	def load_redact_rules(log_root: Path) -> list[tuple[re.Pattern[str], str]]:
87	    custom_path = log_root / "redact.json"
88	    if not custom_path.exists():
89	        return DEFAULT_REDACT_RULES
90	    try:
91	        custom = json.loads(custom_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
92	        compiled = []
93	        for r in custom:
94	            flags = 0
95	            if "i" in r.get("flags", ""):
96	                flags |= re.IGNORECASE
97	            compiled.append((re.compile(r["pattern"], flags), r.get("replacement", "***REDACTED***")))
98	        return [*DEFAULT_REDACT_RULES, *compiled]
99	    except Exception:
100	        return DEFAULT_REDACT_RULES
101	
102	
103	def redact_value(value: Any, rules: list[tuple[re.Pattern[str], str]], counter: list[int]) -> Any:
104	    if isinstance(value, str):
105	        out = value
106	        for pattern, replacement in rules:
107	            new_out, n = pattern.subn(replacement, out)
108	            counter[0] += n
109	            out = new_out
110	        return out
111	    if isinstance(value, list):
112	        return [redact_value(v, rules, counter) for v in value]
113	    if isinstance(value, dict):
114	        return {k: redact_value(v, rules, counter) for k, v in value.items()}
115	    return value

118	def load_transcript(transcript_path: Path) -> list[dict] | None:
119	    if not transcript_path.exists():
120	        return None
121	    try:
122	        events = []
123	        for line in transcript_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
124	            line = line.strip()
125	            if not line:
126	                continue
127	            try:
128	                events.append(json.loads(line))
129	            except json.JSONDecodeError:
130	                continue
131	        return events
132	    except Exception:
133	        return None

135	
136	def expand_claude_event(raw_event: dict, fallback_ts: str) -> list[dict]:
137	    """Convert one Claude Code transcript event to zero-or-more contest events.
138	
139	    Schema (verified against real ~/.claude/projects/*.jsonl):
140	      - top-level type: user / assistant / system / progress / file-history-snapshot / ...
141	      - real conversation lives in .message
142	      - assistant.message.content is a list of blocks: text / thinking / tool_use
143	      - user.message.content is str (plain input) OR list (containing tool_result blocks)
144	      - tool_result blocks carry tool_use_id pairing back to assistant tool_use
145	    """
146	    out: list[dict] = []
147	    top_type = raw_event.get("type")
148	    ts = raw_event.get("timestamp") or fallback_ts
149	
150	    if top_type in ("progress", "file-history-snapshot", "queue-operation", "permission-mode", "agent-name", "summary", "attachment"):
151	        return out
152	
153	    msg = raw_event.get("message") or {}
154	    role = (msg.get("role") or top_type or "system").lower()
155	    content = msg.get("content")
156	    model = msg.get("model")
157	    usage = msg.get("usage") or {}
158	    tokens_in = usage.get("input_tokens")
159	    tokens_out = usage.get("output_tokens")
160	
161	    common = {"ts": ts}
162	    if model:
163	        common["model"] = model
164	    if isinstance(tokens_in, int):
165	        common["tokens_in"] = tokens_in
166	    if isinstance(tokens_out, int):
167	        common["tokens_out"] = tokens_out
168	
169	    if isinstance(content, str):
170	        if content:
171	            out.append({**common, "role": role, "text": content})
172	        return out
173	
174	    if not isinstance(content, list):
175	        return out
176	
177	    for block in content:
178	        if not isinstance(block, dict):
179	            continue
180	        bt = block.get("type")
181	        if bt in SKIP_BLOCK_TYPES:
182	            continue
183	        if bt == "text":
184	            text = block.get("text", "")
185	            if text:
186	                out.append({**common, "role": role, "text": text})
187	        elif bt == "thinking":
188	            thinking = block.get("thinking") or block.get("text") or ""
189	            if thinking:
190	                out.append({**common, "role": role, "thinking": thinking})
191	        elif bt == "tool_use":
192	            out.append({
193	                **common,
194	                "role": "tool",
195	                "tool_name": block.get("name", "unknown"),
196	                "tool_call_id": block.get("id", f"call_{len(out)}"),
197	                "input": block.get("input"),
198	                "output": None,
199	            })
200	        elif bt == "tool_result":
201	            tool_call_id = block.get("tool_use_id", f"call_{len(out)}")
202	            result_content = block.get("content")
203	            out.append({
204	                **common,
205	                "role": "tool",
206	                "tool_name": "<result>",
207	                "tool_call_id": tool_call_id,
208	                "input": None,
209	                "output": result_content,
210	                "is_error": bool(block.get("is_error")),
211	            })
212	        else:
213	            out.append({
214	                **common,
215	                "role": role,
216	                "text": f"[unsupported block type: {bt}]",
217	                "metadata": {"dropped": True, "original_type": bt},
218	            })
219	    return out
220

222	def append_events(jsonl_path: Path, events: list[dict], session_id: str, team_id: str, github_login: str, tool: str, start_seq: int, redact_rules) -> dict:
223	    seq = start_seq
224	    redacted_total = 0
225	    lines = []
226	    for ev in events:
227	        counter = [0]
228	        redacted = redact_value(ev, redact_rules, counter)
229	        redacted_total += counter[0]
230	        out = {
231	            "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
232	            "session_id": session_id,
233	            "team_id": team_id,
234	            "github_login": github_login,
235	            "tool": tool,
236	            "seq": seq,
237	            **redacted,
238	        }
239	        if counter[0] > 0:
240	            out["redacted_count"] = counter[0]
241	        lines.append(json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False))
242	        seq += 1
243	    if not lines:
244	        return {"written": 0, "last_seq": start_seq - 1, "redacted": 0}
245	    jsonl_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
246	    with jsonl_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
247	        f.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
248	    return {"written": len(lines), "last_seq": seq - 1, "redacted": redacted_total}

264	        if data.get("team_id") != team_id:

266	                         f"manifest.team_id={data.get('team_id')} but TEAM_ID env={team_id}")
267	        if data.get("github_login") and data.get("github_login") != github_login:

269	                         f"manifest.github_login={data.get('github_login')} but detected={github_login}")
270	        return data

273	        return {

275	            "team_id": team_id,
276	            "github_login": github_login,
277	            "generator": f"{tool}-collector@{VERSION}",

285	    manifest["team_id"] = team_id

296	def get_repo_root() -> Path | None:
297	    try:
298	        out = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"], stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)
299	        return Path(out.strip())
300	    except Exception:

302	
303	
304	# Privacy gate: only collect inside an openvela workspace, identified by a
305	# `.repo/` dir at the workspace root. Sessions outside it (personal projects)
306	# must never be collected. Returns the workspace root, or None if not inside.
307	def find_openvela_workspace_root(start_dir: Path) -> Path | None:
308	    try:
309	        cur = Path(start_dir).resolve()
310	        for candidate in [cur, *cur.parents]:
311	            if (candidate / ".repo").is_dir():
312	                return candidate
313	    except Exception:
314	        return None
315	    return None
316

318	def get_fallback_author() -> tuple[str, str]:
319	    fallback = os.environ.get("LOG_FALLBACK_EMAIL", "")
320	    if fallback:
321	        return fallback.split("@")[0], fallback
322	    ssh_dir = Path.home() / ".ssh"
323	    try:
324	        for f in ssh_dir.glob("*.pub"):
325	            content = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
326	            parts = content.split()
327	            comment = parts[-1] if parts else ""
328	            if "@" in comment and not comment.startswith("ssh-"):
329	                return comment.split("@")[0], comment
330	    except Exception:
331	        pass
332	    return "contest-collector", "contest-collector@auto-commit.local"
333	
334	
335	def process_claude_stdin(stdin_data: dict, tool: str, team_id: str) -> int:
336	    """Claude Code Stop / SessionEnd hook stdin payload format:
337	        {
338	          "session_id": "...",
339	          "cwd": "...",
340	          "transcript_path": "/abs/path/.jsonl",
341	          "hook_event_name": "Stop" | "SessionEnd",
342	          ...
343	        }
344	    Codex Stop hook stdin payload schema is shared with Claude Code (sso_id/cwd/transcript_path use the same names).
345	    """
346	    session_id = stdin_data.get("session_id") or stdin_data.get("sessionId")
347	    cwd = stdin_data.get("cwd") or os.getcwd()
348	    transcript_path_str = stdin_data.get("transcript_path") or stdin_data.get("transcriptPath")
349	
350	    if not session_id:
351	        sys.stderr.write("[session-log] missing session_id in stdin payload\n")
352	        return 1
353	
354	    repo_root = get_repo_root() or Path(cwd)
355	
356	    workspace_root = find_openvela_workspace_root(repo_root)
357	    if workspace_root is None:
358	        sys.stderr.write(
359	            "[session-log] not inside an openvela workspace (no .repo/ found); "
360	            "collection disabled for this session.\n"
361	        )
362	        return 0
363	
364	    login_result = resolve_github_login(repo_root)
365	    if not login_result[0]:
366	        sys.stderr.write(
367	            "[session-log] FATAL: cannot detect GITHUB_LOGIN. "
368	            "Set GITHUB_LOGIN env, or run 'gh auth login', or fix git remote URL.\n"
369	        )
370	        return 2
371	    github_login = login_result[0]
372	
373	    log_root = Path(os.environ.get("SESSION_LOG_DIR")
374	                    or (Path.home() / ".claude" / "contest-collector-staging"))
375	    member_dir = log_root / github_login
376	    try:
377	        member_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
378	    except Exception as e:
379	        sys.stderr.write(f"[session-log] cannot create member dir {member_dir}: {e}\n")
380	        return 1
381	
382	    if not transcript_path_str:
383	        sys.stderr.write(
384	            "[session-log] skipping: stdin has no transcript_path "
385	            f"(event={stdin_data.get('hook_event_name', '?')}, "
386	            f"session={session_id[:12] if session_id else '?'})\n"
387	        )
388	        return 0
389	    transcript_path = Path(transcript_path_str)
390	
391	    raw_events = load_transcript(transcript_path)
392	    if raw_events is None:
393	        report_error(member_dir, "transcript_unreadable", {"tool": tool, "transcript_path": str(transcript_path)},
394	                     f"cannot read or parse transcript at {transcript_path}")
395	        return 1
396	
397	    manifest = read_manifest(member_dir, team_id, github_login, tool)
398	    entry = next((s for s in manifest.get("sessions", []) if s.get("session_id") == session_id), None)
399	    is_new = entry is None
400	    raw_watermark = entry.get("raw_event_count", 0) if entry else 0
401	    start_seq = entry.get("event_count", 0) if entry else 0
402	
403	    new_raw = raw_events[raw_watermark:]
404	    if not new_raw:
405	        return 0
406	
407	    contest_events = []
408	    for raw in new_raw:
409	        contest_events.extend(expand_claude_event(raw, fallback_ts=iso_now()))
410	
411	    if not contest_events:
412	        if entry is not None:
413	            entry["raw_event_count"] = len(raw_events)
414	            write_manifest(member_dir, manifest, team_id, github_login, tool)
415	        return 0
416	
417	    redact_rules = load_redact_rules(member_dir)
418	    if entry and entry.get("file_path"):
419	        existing_rel = entry["file_path"].split(f"{github_login}/", 1)[-1] \
420	            if f"{github_login}/" in entry["file_path"] else None
421	        if existing_rel:
422	            jsonl_path = member_dir / existing_rel
423	        else:
424	            jsonl_path = member_dir / local_date() / f"{tool}__{session_id}.jsonl"
425	    else:
426	        jsonl_path = member_dir / local_date() / f"{tool}__{session_id}.jsonl"
427	
428	    try:
429	        result = append_events(jsonl_path, contest_events, session_id, team_id, github_login, tool, start_seq, redact_rules)
430	    except Exception as e:
431	        report_error(member_dir, "jsonl_append", {"tool": tool, "jsonl_path": str(jsonl_path)}, e)
432	        return 1
433	
434	    new_event_count = (entry.get("event_count", 0) if entry else 0) + result["written"]
435	    rel_path = f"logs/{github_login}/{local_date()}/{tool}__{session_id}.jsonl"
436	
437	    if is_new:
438	        first_ts = (raw_events[0].get("timestamp") if raw_events else None) or iso_now()
439	        entry = {
440	            "session_id": session_id,
441	            "tool": tool,
442	            "started_at": first_ts,
443	            "last_event_at": iso_now(),
444	            "event_count": new_event_count,
445	            "raw_event_count": len(raw_events),
446	            "file_path": rel_path,
447	            "collection_mode": "cli",
448	            "health": "ok",
449	        }
450	        last_assistant = next((e for e in reversed(raw_events) if (e.get("message") or {}).get("model")), None)
451	        if last_assistant:
452	            model = last_assistant.get("message", {}).get("model")
453	            if model:
454	                entry["model"] = model
455	        manifest.setdefault("sessions", []).append(entry)
456	    else:
457	        entry["last_event_at"] = iso_now()
458	        entry["event_count"] = new_event_count
459	        entry["raw_event_count"] = len(raw_events)
460	        entry["file_path"] = rel_path
461	        if result["redacted"] > 0:
462	            entry["redacted_count_total"] = entry.get("redacted_count_total", 0) + result["redacted"]
463	
464	    write_manifest(member_dir, manifest, team_id, github_login, tool)
465	
466	    auto_export_to_repo(
467	        repo_root, member_dir, jsonl_path, session_id,
468	        team_id, github_login, tool,
469	    )
470	
471	    sys.stderr.write(
472	        f"[session-log] captured {result['written']} event(s) -> {rel_path}\n"
473	    )
474	
475	    return 0

478	def auto_export_to_repo(repo_root: Path, staging_member_dir: Path, staging_jsonl: Path,
479	                        session_id: str, team_id: str, github_login: str, tool: str) -> None:
480	    try:
481	        dest_member = repo_root / "logs" / github_login
482	        dest_jsonl = dest_member / staging_jsonl.relative_to(staging_member_dir)
483	        dest_jsonl.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
484	        shutil.copy2(staging_jsonl, dest_jsonl)
485	
486	        actual_count = sum(1 for line in dest_jsonl.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if line.strip())
487	
488	        staging_manifest = read_manifest(staging_member_dir, team_id, github_login, tool)
489	        src_entry = next((s for s in staging_manifest.get("sessions", [])
490	                          if s.get("session_id") == session_id), None)
491	        if src_entry is None:
492	            return
493	        src_entry["event_count"] = actual_count
494	        src_entry["file_path"] = f"logs/{github_login}/{staging_jsonl.relative_to(staging_member_dir)}"
495	
496	        dest_manifest = read_manifest(dest_member, team_id, github_login, tool)
497	        existing = next((s for s in dest_manifest.get("sessions", [])
498	                         if s.get("session_id") == session_id), None)
499	        if existing:
500	            existing.update(src_entry)
501	        else:
502	            dest_manifest.setdefault("sessions", []).append(src_entry)
503	        write_manifest(dest_member, dest_manifest, team_id, github_login, tool)
504	    except Exception as e:
505	        report_error(staging_member_dir, "auto_export", {"repo_root": str(repo_root), "session_id": session_id}, e)
506	
507	
508	def main() -> int:
509	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
510	    parser.add_argument("--tool", required=True, choices=["claude-code", "codex"])
511	    args = parser.parse_args()
512	
513	    team_id = os.environ.get("TEAM_ID", "").strip()
514	    if not team_id:
515	        sys.stderr.write(
516	            "[session-log] FATAL: TEAM_ID env var not set, refusing to write log to avoid mis-attribution\n"
517	        )
518	        return 2
519	
520	    sys.stderr.write(f"[session-log] tool={args.tool} team={team_id} version={VERSION}\n")
521	
522	    try:
523	        stdin_text = sys.stdin.read()
524	        if not stdin_text.strip():
525	            sys.stderr.write("[session-log] stdin empty, nothing to do\n")
526	            return 0
527	        stdin_data = json.loads(stdin_text)
528	    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
529	        sys.stderr.write(f"[session-log] cannot parse stdin JSON: {e}\n")
530	        return 1
531	
532	    return process_claude_stdin(stdin_data, args.tool, team_id)
533	
534	
535	if __name__ == "__main__":
536	    sys.exit(main())
537

34	def fmt_time(ts: str) -> str:
35	    try:
36	        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
37	        local = dt.astimezone()
38	        return local.strftime("%H:%M:%S")
39	    except Exception:

43	def truncate(s: str, n: int = 200) -> str:
44	    if len(s) <= n:
45	        return s
46	    return s[:n] + f" ... [+{len(s) - n} chars]"

49	def fmt_tool_io(value, max_str: int = 500, indent: int = 4) -> str:
50	    if value is None:

52	    if isinstance(value, str):

54	    try:

56	        return "\n".join(" " * indent + line for line in formatted.split("\n"))
57	    except Exception:
58	        return repr(value)

61	def render_terminal(events: list[dict], use_color: bool = True) -> list[str]:

357	{''.join(body_parts)}

363	</div>""")

365	    parts.append("</body></html>")

368	
369	def load_jsonl(path: Path) -> list[dict]:
370	    events = []
371	    for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
372	        line = line.strip()
373	        if not line:
374	            continue
375	        try:
376	            events.append(json.loads(line))
377	        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
378	            print(f"WARN: bad JSON line in {path}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
379	    return events
380	
381	
382	def find_jsonl_files(target: Path) -> list[Path]:
383	    if target.is_file() and target.suffix == ".jsonl":
384	        return [target]
385	    if target.is_dir():
386	        return sorted(target.rglob("*.jsonl"))
387	    return []
388	
389	
390	def main() -> int:
391	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Render contest session JSONL into human-readable formats")
392	    parser.add_argument("path", help="Path to .jsonl file OR member dir")
393	    parser.add_argument("--format", choices=["terminal", "html", "md"], default="terminal")
394	    parser.add_argument("--no-color", action="store_true", help="disable terminal colors")
395	    parser.add_argument("--out", help="output file path (default: stdout)")
396	    args = parser.parse_args()
397	
398	    target = Path(args.path).resolve()
399	    files = find_jsonl_files(target)
400	    if not files:
401	        print(f"ERROR: no .jsonl files found at {target}", file=sys.stderr)
402	        return 1
403	
404	    output_chunks = []
405	
406	    for jsonl_path in files:
407	        events = load_jsonl(jsonl_path)
408	        if args.format == "terminal":
409	            use_color = not args.no_color and (args.out is None) and sys.stdout.isatty()
410	            output_chunks.extend(render_terminal(events, use_color=use_color))
411	            output_chunks.append("")
412	        elif args.format == "md":
413	            output_chunks.extend(render_markdown(events))
414	            output_chunks.append("\n---\n")
415	        elif args.format == "html":
416	            output_chunks.append(render_html(events))
417	
418	    text = "\n".join(output_chunks)
419	
420	    if args.out:
421	        Path(args.out).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
422	        print(f"Wrote {Path(args.out).resolve()}", file=sys.stderr)
423	    else:
424	        print(text)
425	
426	    return 0
427	
428	
429	if __name__ == "__main__":
430	    sys.exit(main())
431

    value = repr(value)\n    if max_chars and len(value) > max_chars:\n        omitted = len(value) - max_chars\n        value = (value[:max_chars] +\n                 f\"\\n\\n[... {omitted:,} more characters omitted in this \"\n                 f\"Markdown view; full text in ../raw/ ...]\")\n    f = fence(value)\n    return f + \"\\n\" + value + \"\\n\" + f\n\n\ndef render_events(events: list[dict], max_chars: int, with_seq: bool) -> list[str]:\n    lines: list[str] = []\n    for ev in events:\n        role = ev.get(\"role\", \"?\")\n        label = ROLE_LABEL.get(role, role.upper())\n        head = f\"### {label} · {fmt_ts(ev.get('ts', ''))}\"\n        if with_seq:\n            head += f\" · seq={ev.get('seq', '?')}\"\n        meta = []\n        if ev.get(\"model\"):\n            meta.append(f\"model `{ev['model']}`\")\n        if ev.get(\"tokens_in\") is not None:\n            meta.append(f\"tokens {ev.get('tokens_in')}/{ev.get('tokens_out', '?')}\")\n        if meta:\n            head += \"  —  \" + \" · \".join(meta)\n        lines.append(head)\n        lines.append(\"\")\n\n        if role == \"tool\":\n            name = ev.get(\"tool_name\", \"?\")\n            if name == \"<result>\":\n                err = \"  ❌ **ERROR**\" if ev.get(\"is_error\") else \"\"\n                lines.append(f\"**↩ tool result** for `{ev.get('tool_call_id', '')}`{err}\")\n                lines.append(\"\")\n                lines.append(render_block(ev.get(\"output\"), max_chars))\n            else:\n                lines.append(f\"**→ tool call** `{name}` \"\n                             f\"(id `{ev.get('tool_call_id', '')}`)\")\n                lines.append(\"\")\n                lines.append(render_block(ev.get(\"input\"), max_chars))\n                if ev.get(\"output\") is not None:\n                    lines.append(\"**output:**\")\n                    lines.append(\"\")\n                    lines.append(render_block(ev.get(\"output\"), max_chars))\n        else:\n            if ev.get(\"thinking\"):\n                lines.append(\"> 💭 **thinking**\")\n                lines.append(\">\")\n                for ln in ev[\"thinking\"].split(\"\\n\"):\n                    lines.append(f\"> {ln}\")\n                lines.append(\"\")\n            if ev.get(\"text\"):\n                txt = clean_user_text(ev[\"text\"]) if role == \"user\" else ev[\"text\"]\n                if txt:\n                    lines.append(txt)\n                    lines.append(\"\")\n        lines.append(\"---\")\n        lines.append(\"\")\n    return lines\n\n\ndef build_session(spec: dict, max_chars: int) -> dict | None:\n    src: Path = spec[\"src\"]\n    if not src.is_file():\n        print(f\"  !! missing transcript: {src}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return None\n    raw = load_raw(src)\n    events = normalize(raw)\n    ts_list = [e[\"ts\"] for e in events if e.get(\"ts\")]\n    if not ts_list:\n        ts_list = [r.get(\"timestamp\") for r in raw if r.get(\"timestamp\")]\n    return {\n        **spec,\n        \"raw\": raw,\n        \"events\": events,\n        \"start\": min(ts_list) if ts_list else \"\",\n        \"end\": max(ts_list) if ts_list else \"\",\n        \"n_raw\": len(raw),\n        \"n_events\": len(events),\n        \"n_user\": sum(1 for e in events if e.get(\"role\") == \"user\"),\n        \"n_asst\": sum(1 for e in events if e.get(\"role\") == \"assistant\"),\n        \"n_tool\": sum(1 for e in events if e.get(\"role\") == \"tool\"),\n        \"tokens_in\": sum(e.get(\"tokens_in\") or 0 for e in events),\n        \"tokens_out\": sum(e.get(\"tokens_out\") or 0 for e in events),\n    }\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    ap.add_argument(\"--dest\", default=r\"E:\\openvela\\logs\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--max-tool-chars\", type=int, default=0,\n                    help=\"0 = keep tool payloads at full fidelity\")\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    dest = Path(args.dest)\n    (dest / \"raw\").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    (dest / \"sessions\").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    built = []\n    for spec in SESSIONS:\n        s = build_session(spec, args.max_tool_chars)\n        if s:\n            built.append(s)\n\n    built.sort(key=lambda s: s[\"start\"])\n\n    # 1. byte-exact raw backups\n    print(\"raw backups:\")\n    for i, s in enumerate(built, 1):\n        out = dest / \"raw\" / f\"{i:02d}_{s['stage']}__{s['slug']}.jsonl\"\n        shutil.copy2(s[\"src\"], out)\n        print(f\"  {out.name}  ({out.stat().st_size:,} bytes, \"\n              f\"{s['n_raw']:,} records) <- {s['src'].relative_to(HOME)}\")\n\n    # 2. per-session markdown\n    print(\"session markdown:\")\n    for i, s in enumerate(built, 1):\n        body = [\n            f\"# 会话 {i:02d} — {s['stage_cn']}\",\n            \"\",\n            f\"- **session id**: `{s['slug']}`\",\n            f\"- **原始文件**: `raw/{i:02d}_{s['stage']}__{s['slug']}.jsonl`\",\n            f\"- **源码路径**: `{s['src'].relative_to(HOME)}`\",\n            f\"- **时间范围**: {fmt_ts(s['start'])} → {fmt_ts(s['end'])}\",\n            f\"- **原始记录数**: {s['n_raw']:,}\",\n            f\"- **事件数**: {s['n_events']:,} \"\n            f\"(user {s['n_user']}, assistant {s['n_asst']}, tool {s['n_tool']})\",\n            f\"- **token**: in {s['tokens_in']:,} / out {s['tokens_out']:,}\",\n            \"\",\n            \"---\",\n            \"\",\n        ]\n        body += render_events(s[\"events\"], args.max_tool_chars, with_seq=True)\n        p = dest / \"sessions\" / f\"{i:02d}_{s['stage']}__{s['slug']}.md\"\n        p.write_text(\"\\n\".join(body), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        print(f\"  {p.name}  ({p.stat().st_size:,} bytes)\")\n\n    # 3. merged chronological log\n    print(\"merged log:\")\n    merged = [\n        \"# openvela AI 眼镜项目 — 完整 AI 对话日志\",\n        \"\",\n        \"本文件由 `~/.claude/projects/` 下真实保存的 Claude Code 会话记录合并而成，\",\n        \"按事件时间戳升序排列。所有内容均来自本机实际会话，无任何示例或占位数据。\",\n        \"\",\n        f\"- 合并会话数：{len(built)}\",\n        f\"- 事件总数：{sum(s['n_events'] for s in built):,}\",\n        f\"- 时间跨度：{fmt_ts(min(s['start'] for s in built))} → \"\n        f\"{fmt_ts(max(s['end'] for s in built))}\",\n        \"\",\n        \"| # | 阶段 | session id | 起始 | 结束 |\",\n        \"|---|------|-----------|------|------|\",\n    ]\n    for i, s in enumerate(built, 1):\n        merged.append(f\"| {i:02d} | {s['stage_cn']} | `{s['slug']}` | \"\n                      f\"{fmt_ts(s['start'])} | {fmt_ts(s['end'])} |\")\n    merged += [\"\", \"---\", \"\"]\n\n    flat = []\n    for i, s in enumerate(built, 1):\n        for ev in s[\"events\"]:\n            flat.append((ev.get(\"ts\") or s[\"start\"], i, s, ev))\n    flat.sort(key=lambda t: t[0])\n\n    cur = None\n    for ts, idx, s, ev in flat:\n        if idx != cur:\n            cur = idx\n            merged += [\n                \"\",\n                f\"# ═══ 阶段 {idx:02d}：{s['stage_cn']} ═══\",\n                \"\",\n                f\"> session `{s['slug']}` · {fmt_ts(s['start'])} → {fmt_ts(s['end'])}\"\n                f\" · {s['n_events']:,} 事件 · 原文 `raw/{idx:02d}_{s['stage']}__{s['slug']}.jsonl`\",\n                \"\",\n                \"---\",\n                \"\",\n            ]\n        merged += render_events([ev], args.max_tool_chars, with_seq=False)\n\n    p = dest / \"complete-log.md\"\n    p.write_text(\"\\n\".join(merged), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    print(f\"  {p.name}  ({p.stat().st_size:,} bytes)\")\n\n    # machine-readable index for the README / manifest\n    idx = {\n        \"generated_from\": str(PROJECTS),\n        \"sessions\": [\n            {\n                \"index\": i,\n                \"session_id\": s[\"slug\"],\n                \"stage\": s[\"stage\"],\n \"tool_events\": s[\"n_tool\"],\n                \"tokens_in\": s[\"tokens_in\"],\n                \"tokens_out\": s[\"tokens_out\"],\n            }\n            for i, s in enumerate(built, 1)\n        ],\n    }\n    (dest / \"sessions.json\").write_text(\n        json.dumps(idx, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    print(\"  sessions.json\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ ==   "content": "\"\"\"Integrity check: confirm raw/ copies are byte-identical to the source transcripts.\"\"\"\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nsys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding=\"utf-8\")\nHOME = Path.home()\nLOGS = Path(r\"E:\\openvela\\logs\")\n\nidx = json.loads((LOGS / \"sessions.json\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n\n\ndef sha(p: Path) -> str:\n    h = hashlib.sha256()\n    with p.open(\"rb\") as f:\n        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b\"\"):\n            h.update(chunk)\n    return h.hexdigest()\n\n\nok = True\nfor s in idx[\"sessions\"]:\n    i = s[\"index\"]\n    stage = s[\"stage\"]\n    raw = LOGS / \"raw\" / f\"{i:02d}_{stage}__{s['session_id']}.jsonl\"\n    src = Path(s[\"source_transcript\"])\n    if not raw.is_file():\n        print(f\"❌ {raw.name}: MISSING\")\n        ok = False\n        continue\n    a, b = sha(raw), sha(src)\n    same = a == b\n    ok &= same\n    print(f\"{'✅' if same else '❌'} {raw.name}\")\n    print(f\"      raw sha256 = {a}\")\n    print(f\"      src sha256 = {b}\")\n\n# every non-_build file in logs/ must be accounted for\nprint(\"\\n--- full listing ---\")\ntotal = 0\nfor p in sorted(LOGS.rglob(\"*\")):\n    if p.is_file():\n        total += p.stat().st_size\n        print(f\"{p.relative_to(LOGS)!s:<64} {p.stat().st_size:>12,} bytes\")\nprint(f\"\\n{total:,} bytes total\")\nprint(\"\\nRAW INTEGRITY:\", \"ALL MATCH\" if ok else \"MISMATCH FOUND\")\n"

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""
3	contest-log-upload validation tool
4	
5	Validate demo repo's logs/ directory:
6	  1. manifest.json conforms to manifest.schema.json
7	  2. Each line in every .jsonl file conforms to event.schema.json
8	  3. seq within each session is monotonic (tamper detection)

22	from __future__ import annotations

24	import argparse

48	@dataclass
49	class Report:
50	    errors: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
51	    warnings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
52	    files_checked: int = 0
53	    events_checked: int = 0
54	
55	    def err(self, msg: str) -> None:
56	        self.errors.append(msg)
57	
58	    def warn(self, msg: str) -> None:
59	        self.warnings.append(msg)
60	
61	    @property
62	    def has_errors(self) -> bool:
63	        return bool(self.errors)
64

69	
70	def load_schema(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
71	    if not path.exists():
72	        print(f"ERROR: schema not found: {path}", file=sys.stderr)
73	        sys.exit(3)
74	    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
75	        return json.load(f)
76	
77	
78	def validate_event(
79	    event: dict[str, Any],
80	    validator: Draft7Validator,
81	    line_no: int,
82	    file_label: str,
83	    report: Report,
84	) -> None:
85	    for err in validator.iter_errors(event):
86	        # jsonschema's absolute_path is a list of segments, not a JSONPath string;
87	        # we synthesize "$.foo[3].bar" form for human-readable error messages
88	        loc = "$" + "".join(
89	            f"[{p!r}]" if isinstance(p, int) else f".{p}" for p in err.absolute_path
90	        )
91	        report.err(f"{file_label}:{line_no}: {loc}: {err.message}")
92	
93	
94	def validate_jsonl_file(
95	    jsonl_path: Path,
96	    event_validator: Draft7Validator,
97	    expected_team_id: str,
98	    expected_github_login: str,
99	    expected_tool: str,
100	    expected_session_id: str,
101	    report: Report,
102	) -> dict[str, Any]:
103	    """Validate a single .jsonl file. Returns stats {seq_max, ts_min, ts_max, count}."""
104	    stats = {
105	        "seq_max": -1,
106	        "ts_min": None,
107	        "ts_max": None,
108	        "count": 0,
109	        "seq_seen": set(),
110	    }
111	    file_label = str(jsonl_path)
112	
113	    if not jsonl_path.exists():
114	        report.err(f"{file_label}: file does not exist (declared in manifest)")
115	        return stats
116	
117	    try:
118	        content = jsonl_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
119	    except Exception as e:
120	        report.err(f"{file_label}: cannot read: {e}")
121	        return stats
122	
123	    if not content.endswith("\n"):
124	        report.warn(f"{file_label}: file does not end with newline (spec violation)")
125	
126	    last_seq = -1
127	    for line_no, line in enumerate(content.splitlines(), start=1):
128	        if not line.strip():
129	            report.warn(f"{file_label}:{line_no}: empty line in JSONL")
130	            continue
131	
132	        try:
133	            event = json.loads(line)
134	        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
135	            report.err(f"{file_label}:{line_no}: invalid JSON: {e.msg}")
136	            continue
137	
138	        if not isinstance(event, dict):
139	            report.err(f"{file_label}:{line_no}: expected object, got {type(event).__name__}")
140	            continue
141	
142	        validate_event(event, event_validator, line_no, file_label, report)
143	
144	        if event.get("team_id") and event["team_id"] != expected_team_id:
145	            report.err(
146	                f"{file_label}:{line_no}: team_id mismatch: got {event['team_id']!r}, "
147	                f"expected {expected_team_id!r}"
148	            )
149	        if event.get("github_login") and event["github_login"] != expected_github_login:
150	            report.err(
151	                f"{file_label}:{line_no}: github_login mismatch: got {event['github_login']!r}, "
152	                f"expected {expected_github_login!r}"
153	            )
154	        if event.get("tool") and event["tool"] != expected_tool:
155	            report.err(
156	                f"{file_label}:{line_no}: tool mismatch: got {event['tool']!r}, "
157	                f"expected {expected_tool!r}"
158	            )
159	        if event.get("session_id") and event["session_id"] != expected_session_id:
160	            report.err(
161	                f"{file_label}:{line_no}: session_id mismatch: got {event['session_id']!r}, "
162	                f"expected {expected_session_id!r}"
163	            )
164	
165	        if "seq" in event:
166	            seq = event["seq"]
167	            if seq in stats["seq_seen"]:
168	                report.err(
169	                    f"{file_label}:{line_no}: duplicate seq={seq} (anti-cheat violation)"
170	                )
171	            stats["seq_seen"].add(seq)
172	            if seq <= last_seq:
173	                report.err(
174	                    f"{file_label}:{line_no}: seq={seq} not monotonically increasing "
175	                    f"(previous={last_seq}, anti-cheat violation)"
176	                )
177	            last_seq = max(last_seq, seq)
178	            stats["seq_max"] = max(stats["seq_max"], seq)
179	
180	        if "ts" in event:
181	            ts = event["ts"]
182	            if stats["ts_min"] is None or ts < stats["ts_min"]:
183	                stats["ts_min"] = ts
184	            if stats["ts_max"] is None or ts > stats["ts_max"]:
185	                stats["ts_max"] = ts
186	
187	        stats["count"] += 1
188	        report.events_checked += 1
189	
190	    report.files_checked += 1
191	    return stats
192	
193	
194	def _validate_one_member(
195	    member_dir: Path,
196	    repo_root: Path,
197	    manifest_validator: Draft7Validator,
198	    event_validator: Draft7Validator,
199	    report: Report,
200	) -> None:
201	    manifest_path = member_dir / "manifest.json"
202	
203	    if not manifest_path.exists():
204	        report.err(f"{manifest_path}: manifest.json not found in member directory")
205	        return
206	
207	    try:
208	        manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
209	    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
210	        report.err(f"{manifest_path}: invalid JSON: {e}")
211	        return
212	
213	    for err in manifest_validator.iter_errors(manifest):
214	        loc = "$" + "".join(
215	            f"[{p!r}]" if isinstance(p, int) else f".{p}" for p in err.absolute_path
216	        )
217	        report.err(f"{manifest_path}: {loc}: {err.message}")
218	
219	    if "team_id" not in manifest or "sessions" not in manifest:
220	        return
221	
222	    team_id = manifest["team_id"]
223	    github_login = manifest.get("github_login", "")
224	    if github_login != member_dir.name:
225	        report.err(
226	            f"{manifest_path}: manifest.github_login={github_login!r} but parent "
227	            f"directory is {member_dir.name!r}"
228	        )
229	
230	    declared_files: set[Path] = set()
231	
232	    for idx, session in enumerate(manifest.get("sessions", [])):
233	        if not isinstance(session, dict):
234	            continue
235	        rel_path = session.get("file_path", "")
236	        sid = session.get("session_id", f"<unknown-{idx}>")
237	        tool = session.get("tool", "")
238	
239	        # file_path is "logs/<github_login>/<date>/<tool>__<sid>.jsonl" relative
240	        # to the demo repo root. We try multiple resolution bases for robustness.
241	        candidates = [
242	            repo_root / rel_path,
243	            member_dir.parent.parent / rel_path,
244	            member_dir / rel_path.removeprefix(f"logs/{member_dir.name}/"),
245	        ]
246	        jsonl_path = None
247	        for c in candidates:
248	            if c.exists():
249	                jsonl_path = c
250	                break
251	        if jsonl_path is None:
252	            report.err(
253	                f"{manifest_path}: session[{idx}] {sid!r}: declared file_path "
254	                f"{rel_path!r} does not exist"
255	            )
256	            continue
257	
258	        declared_files.add(jsonl_path.resolve())
259	
260	        stats = validate_jsonl_file(
261	            jsonl_path, event_validator, team_id, github_login, tool, sid, report
262	        )
263	
264	        declared_count = session.get("event_count", -1)
265	        if declared_count != stats["count"]:
266	            report.err(
267	                f"{manifest_path}: session[{idx}] {sid!r}: event_count mismatch: "
268	                f"manifest={declared_count}, actual={stats['count']}"
269	            )
270	
271	    for jsonl_path in member_dir.rglob("*.jsonl"):
272	        if jsonl_path.resolve() not in declared_files:
273	            report.warn(
274	                f"{jsonl_path}: orphan file (exists but not in manifest.sessions)"
275	            )
276	
277	
278	def validate_manifest(
279	    logs_dir: Path,
280	    manifest_validator: Draft7Validator,
281	    event_validator: Draft7Validator,
282	    report: Report,
283	) -> None:
284	    # Two invocation modes are supported:
285	    #   1. logs_dir = <repo>/logs/        → iterate member subdirs
286	    #   2. logs_dir = <repo>/logs/<login> → validate that single member only
287	    if (logs_dir / "manifest.json").exists():
288	        repo_root = logs_dir.parent.parent
289	        _validate_one_member(logs_dir, repo_root, manifest_validator, event_validator, report)
290	        return
291	
292	    repo_root = logs_dir.parent
293	    member_dirs = [d for d in logs_dir.iterdir() if d.is_dir() and (d / "manifest.json").exists()]
294	    if not member_dirs:
295	        report.err(f"{logs_dir}: no member directories with manifest.json found")
296	        return
297	    for member_dir in sorted(member_dirs):
298	        _validate_one_member(member_dir, repo_root, manifest_validator, event_validator, report)
299	
300	
301	def main() -> int:
302	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
303	        description="Validate contest session log directory"
304	    )
305	    parser.add_argument(
306	        "logs_dir",
307	        help="Path to demo repo logs/ directory",
308	    )
309	    parser.add_argument(
310	        "--quiet", "-q", action="store_true", help="Only report errors, not warnings"
311	    )
312	    args = parser.parse_args()
313	
314	    logs_dir = Path(args.logs_dir).resolve()
315	    if not logs_dir.is_dir():
316	        print(f"ERROR: not a directory: {logs_dir}", file=sys.stderr)
317	        return 3
318	
319	    event_schema = load_schema(EVENT_SCHEMA_PATH)
320	    manifest_schema = load_schema(MANIFEST_SCHEMA_PATH)
321	    event_validator = Draft7Validator(event_schema)
322	    manifest_validator = Draft7Validator(manifest_schema)
323	
324	    report = Report()
325	    validate_manifest(logs_dir, manifest_validator, event_validator, report)
326	
327	    print(f"=== contest-log-upload validation ===")
328	    print(f"Logs dir:       {logs_dir}")
329	    print(f"Files checked:  {report.files_checked}")
330	    print(f"Events checked: {report.events_checked}")
331	
332	    if report.errors:
333	        print(f"\n❌ ERRORS ({len(report.errors)}):")
334	        for e in report.errors:
335	            print(f"  • {e}")
336	
337	    if report.warnings and not args.quiet:
338	        print(f"\n⚠️  WARNINGS ({len(report.warnings)}):")
339	        for w in report.warnings:
340	            print(f"  • {w}")
341	
342	    if report.has_errors:
343	        print("\n❌ FAILED")
344	        return 1
345	    if report.has_warnings:
346	        print("\n⚠️  PASSED WITH WARNINGS")
347	        return 2
348	    print("\n✅ ALL OK")
349	    return 0
350	
351	
352	if __name__ == "__main__":
353	    sys.exit(main())
354
